	•	SUPARI 

🔥 SEO TITLE (Meta Title)
Supari Processing Plant & Machinery Manufacturer | Betelnut Processing Line

🔥 META DESCRIPTION
Complete Supari (Betelnut) processing plant solutions including cleaning, cutting, grading, roasting, and packaging. Customized turnkey machinery for pan masala and mouth freshener industries.

🏭 H1 HEADING
Supari (Betelnut) Processing Plant & Machinery Solutions

🧠 INTRODUCTION (SEO + POSITIONING)
Supari (betelnut) processing plays a critical role in the production of pan masala, mouth fresheners, and flavored areca nut products. High-quality processing requires precision in cleaning, cutting, drying, roasting, and grading to achieve the desired taste, texture, and consistency.
ART Mechatronics offers complete turnkey solutions for Supari processing plants, delivering fully automated, customized systems designed for high efficiency, hygiene, and consistent product quality. Our solutions are engineered to handle various types of raw betelnut and produce uniform output suitable for premium market applications.

⚙️ COMPLETE SUPARI PROCESS FLOW
1. Raw Supari Feeding & Pre-Cleaning
Raw betelnut is first fed into the system where initial impurities such as dust, leaves, and light foreign particles are removed.
Machine Used:
	•	Pre Cleaner Machine 
👉 Ensures removal of large and light impurities before further processing.

2. Fine Cleaning & Destoning
The material is then passed through advanced cleaning systems to remove stones, sand, and heavy contaminants.
Machines Used:
	•	Destoner Machine 
	•	Cyclone Separator 
👉 Critical step to protect downstream machinery and ensure product purity.

3. Supari Cutting / Size Reduction
Clean betelnuts are processed through cutting machines to achieve desired size and shape depending on product requirement (chips, granules, etc.).
Machines Used:
	•	Supari Cutting Machine 
	•	Betelnut Slicer 
👉 Precision cutting ensures uniform size for consistent mixing and roasting.

4. Grading & Air Classification
After cutting, the material is graded to separate different sizes and remove fine dust or unwanted particles.
Machines Used:
	•	Vibratory Grader 
	•	Air Classifier 
👉 Ensures uniformity and improves final product quality.

5. Roasting / Drying Process
The graded supari is roasted or dried to reduce moisture content and enhance flavor, texture, and shelf life.
Machines Used:
	•	Supari Roaster / Dryer 
	•	Hot Air Generator System 
👉 Controlled temperature ensures perfect crunchiness and consistency.

6. Cooling Process
Post roasting, the product is cooled to stabilize moisture and prevent condensation.
Machines Used:
	•	Cooling Conveyor / System 
👉 Maintains product integrity before further processing.

7. Flavoring & Mixing (Optional)
Depending on the final product, supari is mixed with flavors, perfumes, and additives.
Machines Used:
	•	Ribbon Blender / Paddle Mixer 
	•	Coating Drum 
👉 Ensures uniform coating and flavor distribution.

8. Final Sieving & Quality Check
Final screening is done to remove any inconsistencies and ensure premium quality output.
Machines Used:
	•	Vibro Sifter 

9. Packaging
Finished product is packed into pouches or containers using automated packaging systems.
Machines Used:
	•	Form Fill Seal (FFS) Machines 
	•	Pouch Packing Machines 
👉 High-speed packing suitable for commercial-scale production.

⚙️ MACHINERY REQUIRED FOR SUPARI PROCESSING PLANT
	•	Pre Cleaner 
	•	Destoner 
	•	Cyclone Separator 
	•	Supari Cutting Machine 
	•	Vibratory Grader 
	•	Air Classifier 
	•	Roaster / Dryer 
	•	Cooling System 
	•	Mixer / Blender 
	•	Vibro Sifter 
	•	Packaging Machine 

🏗️ TURNKEY SUPARI PLANT SOLUTIONS
ART Mechatronics provides complete end-to-end turnkey solutions for Supari processing plants, including:
	•	Plant design & layout planning 
	•	Machinery manufacturing & supply 
	•	Automation & control systems 
	•	Installation & commissioning 
	•	After-sales service & maintenance 
We customize each plant based on:
	•	Production capacity 
	•	Product type (plain, flavored, roasted) 
	•	Available space 
	•	Level of automation 

💎 WHY CHOOSE ART MECHATRONICS FOR SUPARI PROCESSING
	•	30+ years of engineering expertise 
	•	Proven experience in pan masala & mouth freshener industry 
	•	High-efficiency, low-maintenance machinery 
	•	Custom-built solutions for different product types 
	•	Global export capability

	•	Mouth Freshener Production

	•	PAN MASALA

🔥 SEO TITLE (Meta Title)
Pan Masala Manufacturing Plant & Machinery | Turnkey Processing & Packaging Solutions

🔥 META DESCRIPTION
Complete pan masala manufacturing plant solutions including supari processing, blending, flavoring, dust control, and high-speed pouch packaging. Customized turnkey systems for global manufacturers.

🏭 H1 HEADING
Pan Masala Manufacturing Plant & Machinery Solutions

🧠 INTRODUCTION (SEO + POSITIONING)
Pan masala manufacturing is a highly specialized process that requires precision in ingredient handling, uniform mixing, controlled flavoring, and high-speed packaging. Consistency in taste, texture, and hygiene is critical to maintain product quality and brand value.
ART Mechatronics offers complete turnkey solutions for pan masala manufacturing plants, covering everything from raw material processing to final packaging. Our systems are designed for high-speed production, dust-free operation, and uniform product quality, making them ideal for large-scale commercial manufacturing.

⚙️ COMPLETE PAN MASALA PROCESS FLOW
1. Raw Material Handling & Feeding
Raw materials such as supari, catechu, flavoring agents, and other ingredients are received, stored, and fed into the processing line.
Machines Used:
	•	Material Handling Systems 
	•	Storage Bins & Feeders 
👉 Ensures smooth and controlled material flow.

2. Supari Processing
Betelnut is processed through cleaning, cutting, grading, and roasting to achieve the desired size, texture, and moisture level.
Machines Used:
	•	Pre Cleaner 
	•	Destoner 
	•	Supari Cutting Machine 
	•	Grader 
	•	Roaster / Dryer 
👉 (Link to Supari Processing Page)

3. Ingredient Preparation
Other ingredients such as catechu, flavor powders, and additives are processed and prepared for mixing.
Machines Used:
	•	Pulverizer / Grinder 
	•	Sieving Machines 
👉 Ensures fine and uniform ingredient consistency.

4. Blending & Mixing
All ingredients are blended in precise ratios to achieve consistent taste, aroma, and texture.
Machines Used:
	•	Ribbon Blender 
	•	Paddle Mixer 
	•	Flavor Mixing Systems 
👉 Critical stage for product uniformity and quality.

5. Coating & Flavor Application
Liquid flavors, perfumes, and additives are evenly applied to the mixture.
Machines Used:
	•	Coating Drum 
	•	Spray System 
👉 Ensures uniform coating and enhanced product appeal.

6. Drying / Conditioning
The blended product is conditioned or lightly dried to stabilize moisture and improve shelf life.
Machines Used:
	•	Tray Dryer / Continuous Dryer 

7. Dust Collection & Pollution Control (VERY IMPORTANT)
Pan masala production generates fine dust, especially during mixing and packaging.
Systems Used:
	•	Bag House Dust Collector 
	•	Cyclone Separator 
	•	Centralized Dust Extraction System 
👉 Ensures:
	•	Clean working environment 
	•	Product recovery 
	•	Compliance with safety standards 

8. High-Speed Packaging
Final product is packed into pouches at high speed using automated systems.
Machines Used:
	•	Rotary Pouch Packing Machine 
	•	Form Fill Seal (FFS) Machine 
	•	Multi-Track Packing Systems 
👉 Capable of packing 2000–2500 pouches per minute

9. Secondary Packing (Optional)
Packed pouches are grouped and packed into mono cartons.
Machines Used:
	•	Cartoning Machines 
	•	Conveyor Systems 

⚙️ MACHINERY REQUIRED FOR PAN MASALA PLANT
	•	Supari Processing Line 
	•	Pulverizers / Grinders 
	•	Ribbon Blender / Paddle Mixer 
	•	Coating Drum 
	•	Drying Systems 
	•	Dust Collection System 
	•	High-Speed Packing Machines 
	•	Conveyors & Material Handling Systems

ART Mechatronics provides complete end-to-end turnkey solutions for pan masala plants, including:

	•	Dust control & air handling systems

	•	SWEET SUPARI

🔥 SEO TITLE (Meta Title)
Sweet Supari Processing Plant & Machinery | Flavored Betelnut Manufacturing Solutions

🔥 META DESCRIPTION
Complete sweet supari processing plant solutions including cutting, roasting, coating, flavoring, drying, and packaging. Turnkey machinery for premium mouth freshener manufacturing.

🏭 H1 HEADING
Sweet Supari (Flavored Betelnut) Processing Plant & Machinery

🧠 INTRODUCTION (SEO + POSITIONING)
Sweet supari, also known as flavored betelnut, is a popular mouth freshener product widely consumed across global markets. The manufacturing process requires precision in cutting, roasting, coating, flavoring, and drying to achieve consistent taste, texture, and visual appeal.
ART Mechatronics provides complete turnkey solutions for sweet supari processing plants, delivering automated and customized machinery systems designed for uniform coating, controlled flavor infusion, and high-quality output. Our solutions ensure consistent product quality, hygiene, and scalability for commercial production.

⚙️ COMPLETE SWEET SUPARI PROCESS FLOW
1. Raw Betelnut Cleaning & Preparation
Raw supari is cleaned to remove dust, stones, and unwanted impurities before processing.
Machines Used:
	•	Pre Cleaner 
	•	Destoner 
	•	Cyclone Separator 
👉 Ensures clean raw material for high-quality processing.

2. Supari Cutting / Size Reduction
Clean betelnuts are cut into required sizes such as chips, granules, or small pieces depending on product type.
Machines Used:
	•	Supari Cutting Machine 
	•	Betelnut Slicer 
👉 Uniform cutting is essential for consistent coating and flavor absorption.

3. Grading & Separation
Cut supari is graded to separate different sizes and remove fines.
Machines Used:
	•	Vibratory Grader 
	•	Air Classifier 
👉 Ensures uniform batch quality and improved processing efficiency.

4. Roasting / Drying
The supari is roasted or dried to reduce moisture content and enhance crunchiness and shelf life.
Machines Used:
	•	Supari Roaster / Dryer 
	•	Hot Air Generator 
👉 Controlled temperature ensures consistent roasting without burning.

5. Cooling Process
The roasted supari is cooled before flavor application to stabilize the product.
Machines Used:
	•	Cooling Conveyor / Cooling System 

6. Coating & Flavoring (CORE PROCESS)
This is the most critical stage where sweeteners, flavors, colors, and perfumes are applied to the supari.
Machines Used:
	•	Coating Drum 
	•	Flavor Spray System 
	•	Ribbon Blender / Paddle Mixer 
👉 Ensures:
	•	Uniform coating 
	•	Consistent taste 
	•	Attractive appearance 

7. Drying / Conditioning After Coating
The coated product is dried or conditioned to fix the coating and enhance shelf stability.
Machines Used:
	•	Tray Dryer / Continuous Dryer 

8. Final Grading & Quality Check
The final product is screened to ensure uniform size and quality.
Machines Used:
	•	Vibro Sifter 

9. Packaging
Finished sweet supari is packed into pouches or containers using automated packaging machines.
Machines Used:
	•	Form Fill Seal (FFS) Machine 
	•	Pouch Packing Machine 
👉 Suitable for high-speed commercial production.

⚙️ MACHINERY REQUIRED FOR SWEET SUPARI PLANT
	•	Pre Cleaner 
	•	Destoner 
	•	Cyclone Separator 
	•	Supari Cutting Machine 
	•	Vibratory Grader 
	•	Air Classifier 
	•	Roaster / Dryer 
	•	Cooling System 
	•	Coating Drum 
	•	Mixer / Blender 
	•	Dryer (Post Coating) 
	•	Vibro Sifter 
	•	Packaging Machine

🧠 UPDATED INTRO (IMPORTANT FIX)
Mouth fresheners, commonly known as mukhwas, are widely consumed FMCG products made using a blend of seeds, sugar-coated ingredients, herbs, and flavoring agents. These products are known for their refreshing taste, aroma, and digestive benefits.
ART Mechatronics offers complete turnkey solutions for mouth freshener manufacturing plants, designed for precise mixing, uniform coating, controlled flavor application, and high-speed packaging. 🏭 H1 HEADING
Silver Coated Elaichi (Cardamom) Processing Plant & Machinery Solutions

🧠 INTRODUCTION (SEO + POSITIONING)
Silver coated elaichi (cardamom) is a premium mouth freshener product widely used in the FMCG and confectionery segments. Known for its aromatic flavor and attractive metallic finish, the product requires precision processing, hygienic handling, and uniform coating to maintain its premium appeal.
ART Mechatronics provides complete turnkey solutions for silver coated elaichi processing plants, offering advanced systems for cleaning, grading, coating, drying, polishing, and packaging. Our solutions are designed to ensure uniform coating quality, product safety, and high-end visual finish, suitable for large-scale commercial production.

⚙️ COMPLETE SILVER COATED ELAICHI PROCESS FLOW
1. Raw Elaichi Cleaning & Pre-Processing
Raw cardamom pods are cleaned to remove dust, foreign particles, and impurities.
Machines Used:
	•	Pre Cleaner 
	•	Air Classifier 
👉 Ensures hygienic and clean raw material for further processing.

2. Grading & Size Separation
Elaichi is graded based on size to ensure uniform coating and premium product consistency.
Machines Used:
	•	Vibratory Grader 
	•	Size Grading Machine 
👉 Uniform size improves coating efficiency and appearance.

3. Surface Preparation
The product is prepared for coating by ensuring dryness and surface readiness.
Machines Used:
	•	Drying System (if required) 

4. Silver Coating Process (CORE STAGE)
Edible silver coating is applied uniformly on the elaichi surface to achieve a premium metallic finish.
Machines Used:
	•	Coating Pan / Coating Drum 
	•	Precision Spray System 
👉 Ensures:
	•	Uniform coating 
	•	High visual appeal 
	•	Premium finish quality 

5. Drying & Fixing of Coating
The coated product is dried to stabilize the coating and enhance durability.
Machines Used:
	•	Tray Dryer / Controlled Drying System 

6. Polishing & Finishing
The coated elaichi is polished to enhance shine and final appearance.
Machines Used:
	•	Polishing Drum 
👉 Critical for achieving premium market look.

7. Final Sorting & Quality Inspection
Final inspection is carried out to remove defective or non-uniform pieces.
Machines Used:
	•	Inspection Conveyor 
	•	Vibro Sifter 

8. Packaging
Finished product is packed into pouches, jars, or premium containers using automated systems.
Machines Used:
	•	Pouch Packing Machine 
	•	Jar Filling Machine 
	•	Form Fill Seal (FFS) Machine 
👉 Suitable for high-speed FMCG packaging.

Complete spices processing plant solutions for turmeric, chilli, coriander, pepper, and mixed spices powder. Includes cleaning, grinding, blending, dust control, and packaging machinery.

🏭 H1 HEADING
Spices Powder Processing Plant & Machinery (Turmeric, Chilli, Coriander, Pepper & Masala)

🧠 INTRODUCTION (SEO + POSITIONING)
Spice powder manufacturing is a critical segment in the global food industry, covering products such as turmeric powder, chilli powder, coriander powder, black pepper powder, and blended masala spices. The process requires precision cleaning, controlled grinding, hygienic handling, and uniform blending to maintain natural aroma, color, and flavor.
ART Mechatronics provides complete turnkey solutions for spices processing plants, delivering advanced systems for cleaning, grinding, pulverizing, blending, sieving, and packaging. Our solutions are engineered to ensure high efficiency, minimal product loss, and export-quality spice production.

⚙️ COMPLETE SPICES PROCESS FLOW
1. Raw Material Handling
Whole spices such as turmeric fingers, dried chillies, coriander seeds, black pepper, and other raw materials are received and fed into the processing line.
Machines Used:
	•	Conveyor Systems 
	•	Material Handling Equipment 

2. Cleaning & Destoning (VERY IMPORTANT)
Spices are cleaned to remove dust, stones, metals, and impurities.
Machines Used:
	•	Pre Cleaner 
	•	Destoner 
	•	Magnetic Separator 
	•	Air Classifier 
👉 Critical for:
	•	Food safety 
	•	Equipment protection 
	•	Export-quality standards 

3. Drying (If Required)
Spices are dried to achieve ideal moisture content for grinding.
Machines Used:
	•	Tray Dryer 
	•	Continuous Dryer 
👉 Ensures:
	•	Better grinding efficiency 
	•	Longer shelf life 

4. Grinding / Pulverizing (CORE PROCESS)
Different spices are ground into fine powder with controlled temperature.
Machines Used:
	•	Pulverizer 
	•	Spice Grinding Machine 
	•	Hammer Mill 
👉 Ensures:
	•	Fine particle size 
	•	Color retention 
	•	Aroma preservation 

5. Sieving & Classification
Ground powder is sieved to achieve uniform particle size.
Machines Used:
	•	Vibro Sifter 
	•	Air Classifier 

6. Blending & Mixing (FOR MASALA)
Different spices are blended to create mixed spice powders and masalas.
Machines Used:
	•	Ribbon Blender 
	•	Paddle Mixer 
👉 Ensures:
	•	Uniform mixing 
	•	Consistent flavor profile 

7. Dust Collection & Hygiene Control (CRITICAL)
Fine powder and spice dust are controlled using advanced systems.
Systems Used:
	•	Dust Collector 
	•	Cyclone Separator 
	•	Centralized Dust Extraction System 
👉 Ensures:
	•	Clean environment 
	•	Product recovery 
	•	Food-grade processing 

8. Quality Inspection
Final inspection ensures consistency in color, particle size, and purity.
Machines Used:
	•	Inspection Systems 
	•	Screening Equipment 

9. Packaging
Finished spice powders are packed into pouches, jars, or bulk bags.
Machines Used:
	•	Form Fill Seal (FFS) Machine 
	•	Pouch Packing Machine 
	•	Jar Filling Machine 
👉 Suitable for:
	•	Retail 
	•	Bulk supply 
	•	Export 

🌶️ SPICE-SPECIFIC PROCESSING (SEO BOOST SECTION)
Turmeric Powder Processing
	•	Cleaning → Boiling (optional) → Drying → Grinding → Sieving 👉 Focus on color retention & curcumin quality 

Chilli Powder Processing
	•	Cleaning → Stem cutting → Drying → Grinding → Dust control 👉 Requires strong dust extraction system 

Coriander Powder Processing
	•	Cleaning → Drying → Grinding → Sieving 👉 Focus on aroma retention 

Black Pepper Powder Processing
	•	Cleaning → Drying → Grinding → Fine sieving 👉 Requires controlled grinding temperature 

Mixed Spice / Masala Processing
	•	Individual grinding → Blending → Packaging 👉 Requires precision mixing system 

⚙️ MACHINERY REQUIRED FOR SPICES PLANT
	•	Material Handling Systems 
	•	Pre Cleaner 
	•	Destoner 
	•	Magnetic Separator 
	•	Air Classifier 
	•	Dryer 
	•	Pulverizer / Grinding Machine 
	•	Vibro Sifter 
	•	Ribbon Blender / Paddle Mixer 
	•	Dust Collection System 
	•	Packaging Machines

	•	Cleaning, grinding, and blending systems 
	•	Dust control & air handling systems 
	•	Packaging line integration 
	•	Automation & control panels 
	•	Installation & commissioning 
	•	After-sales support 
We customize solutions based on:
	•	Spice type 
	•	Required fineness 
	•	Production capacity 
	•	Packaging format

🏭 H1 HEADING
Animal Feed Manufacturing Plant & Machinery Solutions (Poultry, Cattle & Aqua Feed)

🧠 INTRODUCTION (SEO + POSITIONING)
Animal feed manufacturing is a large-scale industrial process that involves precise formulation, grinding, mixing, pelletizing, and conditioning to produce nutritionally balanced feed for livestock and aquaculture. Consistency, hygiene, and production efficiency are critical for ensuring high-quality feed output.
ART Mechatronics provides complete turnkey solutions for animal feed manufacturing plants, offering advanced systems for grinding, mixing, pelletizing, cooling, and packaging. Our solutions are engineered for high-capacity production, uniform feed quality, and efficient plant operation, making them ideal for global feed manufacturers.

⚙️ COMPLETE ANIMAL FEED PROCESS FLOW
1. Raw Material Handling & Storage
Raw materials such as grains, oil cakes, additives, vitamins, and minerals are received and stored.
Machines Used:
	•	Material Handling Systems 
	•	Storage Silos / Bins 
👉 Ensures efficient bulk material handling.

2. Cleaning & Pre-Processing
Raw materials are cleaned to remove impurities like dust, stones, and metal particles.
Machines Used:
	•	Pre Cleaner 
	•	Destoner 
	•	Magnetic Separator 
👉 Protects downstream equipment and ensures product purity.

Raw materials are ground into fine particles for uniform mixing.

	•	Hammer Mill 
	•	Pulverizer

	•	Improved digestibility 
	•	Uniform mixing

	•	Micro Dosing System

All ingredients are blended to create a homogeneous feed mixture.
Machines Used:
	•	Ribbon Blender 
	•	Paddle Mixer 
👉 Ensures consistent nutrient distribution.

6. Conditioning (CRITICAL STAGE)
The feed mixture is conditioned using steam or moisture before pelletizing.
Machines Used:
	•	Conditioner 
	•	Steam Injection System 
👉 Improves pellet quality and digestibility.

7. Pelletizing / Feed Formation
The feed is formed into pellets or granules.
Machines Used:
	•	Pellet Mill 
	•	Pelletizing Machine 
👉 Ensures:
	•	Uniform size 
	•	Better handling 
	•	Reduced wastage 

8. Cooling & Drying
Pellets are cooled to stabilize moisture and improve durability.
Machines Used:
	•	Pellet Cooler 
	•	Cooling System 

9. Crumbling (Optional)
Pellets may be broken into smaller sizes for specific applications.
Machines Used:
	•	Crumbler 

10. Screening & Quality Control
Feed is screened to remove fines and ensure uniform size.
Machines Used:
	•	Vibro Sifter 
	•	Screening System 

11. Dust Collection & Air Handling
Fine dust generated during processing is controlled.
Systems Used:
	•	Dust Collector 
	•	Cyclone Separator 
👉 Ensures clean and safe working environment.

12. Packaging
Finished feed is packed into bags or bulk containers.
Machines Used:
	•	Bag Filling Machine 
	•	Weighing System 
	•	Stitching / Sealing Machine 
👉 Suitable for bulk and retail supply.

🏭 H1 HEADING
Peanut Processing Plant & Machinery (Salted, Roasted & Coated Peanuts)

🧠 INTRODUCTION (SEO + POSITIONING)
Peanut processing is a rapidly growing segment in the global snack food industry, covering products such as salted peanuts, roasted peanuts, masala peanuts, and coated peanuts. The manufacturing process requires precise cleaning, controlled roasting or frying, uniform coating, and consistent seasoning to ensure high product quality and consumer appeal.

⚙️ COMPLETE PEANUT PROCESS FLOW
1. Raw Peanut Handling & Feeding
Raw peanuts are received, stored, and fed into the processing system.
Machines Used:
	•	Conveyor Systems 
	•	Feeding Systems 
👉 Ensures smooth and continuous flow.

2. Cleaning & Destoning (CRITICAL STEP)

Machines Used:
	•	Pre Cleaner 
	•	Destoner 
	•	Air Classifier

The manufacturing process requires precise dough preparation, controlled shaping, uniform cooking, and efficient drying or frying to ensure product quality and shelf stability.

⚙️ COMPLETE NOODLE PROCESS FLOW
1. Raw Material Handling
Raw materials such as wheat flour (maida/semolina), water, starch, and additives are received and fed into the system.
Machines Used:
	•	Material Handling Systems 
	•	Storage Silos / Feeders 
👉 Ensures continuous and controlled feeding.

2. Mixing & Dough Preparation (CORE PROCESS)
Flour and water are mixed to form dough with proper elasticity.
Machines Used:
	•	Dough Mixer 
	•	Pre-Mixer 
👉 Ensures:
	•	Uniform hydration 
	•	Consistent dough structure 
	•	High-quality noodle texture 

3. Sheeting / Extrusion (CRITICAL STAGE)
🔸 For Long & Flat Noodles (Hakka, Chowmein):
	•	Dough is passed through rollers to form sheets 
Machines Used:
	•	Sheeting Machine 
	•	Roller System 

🔸 For Round / Extruded Types (Spaghetti, Vermicelli):
	•	Dough is extruded through dies 
Machines Used:
	•	Noodle Extruder 
👉 Ensures:
	•	Uniform thickness 
	•	Consistent shape 

4. Cutting & Shaping
Sheets or extruded dough are cut into required noodle shapes.
Machines Used:
	•	Cutting Machine 
👉 Produces:
	•	Flat noodles 
	•	Round noodles 
	•	Thin vermicelli strands 

5. Steaming / Cooking (IMPORTANT STAGE)
Noodles are partially cooked using steam.
Machines Used:
	•	Steamer 
	•	Continuous Steam Cooker 
👉 Improves:
	•	Texture 
	•	Product stability 

6. Drying or Frying
🔸 For Instant Noodles:
	•	Fried for quick cooking 
Machines Used:
	•	Continuous Fryer 

🔸 For Dried Noodles:
	•	Dried using controlled air 
Machines Used:
	•	Dryer 
	•	Multi-Stage Drying System 
👉 Ensures:
	•	Long shelf life 
	•	Product strength 

7. Cooling
Product is cooled before packaging.
Machines Used:
	•	Cooling Conveyor 

8. Quality Inspection
Final inspection ensures consistency in shape, size, and quality.
Machines Used:
	•	Inspection System 

9. Packaging (HIGH-SPEED FMCG)
Finished noodles are packed into pouches or cartons.
Machines Used:
	•	Form Fill Seal (FFS) Machine 
	•	Weighing System 
	•	Cartoning Machine 
👉 Suitable for retail and bulk packaging.

🧠 INTRODUCTION (SEO + POSITIONING)
Instant noodles are one of the most widely consumed ready-to-cook food products globally, offering convenience, long shelf life, and consistent taste. The manufacturing process involves precise dough preparation, sheeting, steaming, frying, cake forming, and high-speed packaging to achieve uniform quality and efficient production.

⚙️ COMPLETE INSTANT NOODLE PROCESS FLOW
1. Raw Material Handling
Raw materials such as wheat flour, starch, water, and additives are received and fed into the system.
Machines Used:
	•	Material Handling Systems 
	•	Storage Silos / Feeders 
👉 Ensures continuous and controlled input.

2. Mixing & Dough Preparation (CORE PROCESS)
Ingredients are mixed to form uniform dough.
Machines Used:
	•	Dough Mixer 
	•	Pre-Mixer 
👉 Ensures:
	•	Proper hydration 
	•	Consistent dough quality 
	•	Strong gluten structure 

3. Sheeting & Rolling (CRITICAL STAGE)
Dough is passed through rollers to form thin sheets.
Machines Used:
	•	Sheeting Machine 
	•	Roller System 
👉 Ensures uniform thickness and structure.

4. Slitting & Waving
Sheets are cut into noodle strands and shaped into wavy patterns.
Machines Used:
	•	Slitting Machine 
	•	Waving Conveyor 
👉 Creates the characteristic noodle structure.

5. Steaming / Cooking
Noodles are steamed to gelatinize starch and partially cook the product.
Machines Used:
	•	Continuous Steamer 
👉 Improves texture and cooking properties.

6. Cutting & Cake Forming
Noodles are cut and shaped into blocks (cakes).
Machines Used:
	•	Cutting & Forming Machine 
👉 Ensures uniform size and shape.

7. Frying (CORE STAGE FOR INSTANT NOODLES)
Noodle cakes are fried to remove moisture and enable quick cooking.
Machines Used:
	•	Continuous Fryer 
👉 Ensures:
	•	Long shelf life 
	•	Instant rehydration 
	•	Crispy structure 

8. Cooling
Fried noodle cakes are cooled before packaging.
Machines Used:
	•	Cooling Conveyor 

9. Seasoning Sachet Preparation (OPTIONAL INTEGRATION)
Seasoning powder and oil sachets are prepared and packed separately.
Machines Used:
	•	Powder Packing Machine 
	•	Liquid Sachet Packing Machine 

10. Packaging (HIGH-SPEED FMCG)
Noodle cakes and seasoning sachets are packed into retail packs.
Machines Used:
	•	Form Fill Seal (FFS) Machine 
	•	Flow Wrap Machine 
	•	Cartoning Machine 
👉 Enables high-speed retail-ready packaging.

Groundnut (Peanut) Processing Plant & Machinery Solutions

🧠 INTRODUCTION (SEO + POSITIONING)

The manufacturing process requires efficient cleaning, precise shelling, uniform grading, controlled roasting, and hygienic packaging to ensure high-quality output.

🏭 H1 HEADING
Areca Nut (Betelnut / Supari) Processing Plant & Machinery Solutions

🧠 INTRODUCTION (SEO + POSITIONING)
Areca nut processing, also known as betelnut or supari processing, is a specialized agro-processing segment that involves converting raw green areca nut fruit into dried and processed supari products. The process requires efficient dehusking, controlled boiling, precision drying, and uniform cutting to achieve the desired quality, texture, and shelf life.
ART Mechatronics provides complete turnkey solutions for areca nut processing plants, offering advanced systems for dehusking, boiling, drying, cutting, grading, and packaging. Our solutions are engineered for high efficiency, uniform drying, and consistent product quality, making them ideal for large-scale industrial operations.

⚙️ COMPLETE ARECA NUT PROCESS FLOW
1. Raw Areca Nut Handling
Fresh green areca nuts are received and fed into the processing system.
Machines Used:
	•	Material Handling Systems 
	•	Feeding Systems 
👉 Ensures smooth and continuous processing.

2. Dehusking (CRITICAL STEP)
Outer green husk is removed to obtain raw areca nut.
Machines Used:
	•	Areca Nut Dehusker 
👉 Ensures:
	•	Efficient husk removal 
	•	Minimal damage to nut 

3. Washing & Cleaning
Dehusked nuts are cleaned to remove impurities.
Machines Used:
	•	Washing System 
	•	Cleaning Equipment 
👉 Improves product quality before processing.

4. Boiling / Cooking (CORE PROCESS)
Areca nuts are boiled to soften the structure and develop required characteristics.
Machines Used:
	•	Boiling Tank / Cooker 
	•	Steam-Based Heating System 
👉 Ensures:
	•	Proper curing 
	•	Uniform color development 
	•	Improved processing quality 

5. Drying (VERY CRITICAL STAGE – YOUR USP)
Boiled nuts are dried to reduce moisture and achieve required hardness.
Machines Used:
	•	Areca Nut Dryer 
	•	Hot Air Drying System 
	•	Tray Dryer / Continuous Dryer 
👉 Ensures:
	•	Controlled moisture reduction 
	•	Uniform drying 
	•	Crack-free product 

6. Cutting / Slicing
Dried areca nuts are cut into required sizes.
Machines Used:
	•	Betelnut Cutting Machine 
👉 Produces:
	•	Small supari pieces 
	•	Flakes or chips 

7. Grading & Sorting
Cut pieces are graded based on size and quality.
Machines Used:
	•	Grading Machine 
	•	Vibratory Sifter 
👉 Ensures uniform product output.

8. Polishing (OPTIONAL)
Product may be polished for improved appearance.
Machines Used:
	•	Polishing Machine 

9. Dust Collection & Air Handling (IMPORTANT)
Fine dust generated during cutting is controlled.
Systems Used:
	•	Dust Collector 
	•	Cyclone Separator 
👉 Ensures:
	•	Clean working environment 
	•	Product recovery 

10. Packaging
Final product is packed into pouches or bulk bags.
Machines Used:
	•	Pouch Packing Machine 
	•	Weighing System 
👉 Suitable for commercial and export packaging.

Machines Used:

ART Mechatronics provides complete turnkey solutions for flour mill plants, offering advanced systems for cleaning, conditioning, milling, sieving, and packaging. Machines Used:
	•	Material Handling Systems 
	•	Storage Silos 
👉 Ensures efficient bulk handling and storage.

👉 Ensures:

Machines Used:
	•	Purifier 

7. Blending (OPTIONAL)

Machines Used:
	•	Ribbon Blender 

8. Dust Collection & Air Handling (VERY IMPORTANT)
Fine flour dust generated during milling is controlled.
Systems Used:
	•	Dust Collector 
	•	Cyclone Separator 
	•	Centralized Dust Extraction System 
👉 Ensures:
	•	Clean plant environment 
	•	Worker safety 
	•	Product recovery

Final inspection ensures consistent product quality.
Machines Used:
	•	Inspection Systems

Machines Used:

🏭 H1 HEADING
Grain Cleaning, Grading & Packing Plant & Machinery Solutions

🧠 INTRODUCTION (SEO + POSITIONING)
Grain processing units play a critical role in the agricultural supply chain by procuring raw grains directly from farms and converting them into clean, graded, and market-ready products. These units handle grains such as oats, millet, quinoa, barley, wheat, durum, corn, maize, sorghum, and semolina-grade grains, preparing them for wholesale, retail, and export markets.
ART Mechatronics provides complete turnkey solutions for grain cleaning, grading, and packaging plants, offering advanced systems for impurity removal, size grading, polishing, and bulk packaging. Our solutions are designed for high throughput, minimal grain loss, and export-quality processing.

⚙️ COMPLETE GRAIN PROCESS FLOW (POST-HARVEST PROCESSING)
1. Raw Grain Procurement & Intake
Grains are received directly from farms and unloaded into the processing system.
Machines Used:
	•	Intake Hopper 
	•	Material Handling Systems 
👉 Ensures smooth intake and flow.

2. Pre-Cleaning (CRITICAL STEP)
Initial cleaning removes large impurities such as straw, dust, and husk.
Machines Used:
	•	Pre Cleaner 
👉 Ensures removal of coarse impurities.

3. Fine Cleaning & Destoning
Grains are further cleaned to remove stones, sand, and finer impurities.
Machines Used:

The process requires efficient cleaning, precise dehusking, controlled splitting, and polishing to achieve high-quality output with minimal breakage and maximum yield.
ART Mechatronics provides complete turnkey solutions for pulses processing plants, offering advanced systems for cleaning, grading, dehusking, splitting, polishing, and packaging. 🏭 H1 HEADING
Rusk Manufacturing Plant & Machinery Solutions (Toast Biscuit & Double-Baked Production Lines)

🧠 INTRODUCTION (SEO + POSITIONING)
Rusk, also known as toast biscuit, is a popular bakery product known for its long shelf life, crisp texture, and wide consumer demand. It is produced through a double baking process, where bread is first baked, sliced, and then baked again to achieve the desired crunch and durability.
ART Mechatronics provides complete turnkey solutions for rusk manufacturing plants, offering advanced systems for mixing, fermentation, baking, slicing, double baking, and packaging. Our solutions are designed for high-speed production, uniform quality, and efficient large-scale operations for domestic and export markets.

Ingredients such as flour, sugar, yeast, milk powder, and additives are received and fed into the system.
Machines Used:
	•	Material Handling Systems 
	•	Ingredient Dosing System

Machines Used:
	•	Dough Mixer / Spiral Mixer 
👉 Ensures:
	•	Uniform mixing 
	•	Proper gluten development 

3. Fermentation / Proofing (IMPORTANT)

	•	Panning System

Machines Used:
	•	Bread Slicer 
👉 Ensures uniform thickness for even baking.

Sliced bread is baked again to form rusk.
Machines Used:
	•	Rusk Oven / Continuous Baking Oven 
👉 Ensures:
	•	Crisp texture 
	•	Low moisture 
	•	Long shelf life 

9. Cooling
Rusk is cooled before packaging.
Machines Used:
	•	Cooling Conveyor

Finished rusk is packed into pouches.
Machines Used:
	•	Flow Wrap Machine 
	•	Form Fill Seal (FFS) Machine

Pickle and chutney manufacturing is a well-established segment in the global food processing industry, producing products such as mango pickle, mixed pickle, chilli pickle, fruit chutneys, vegetable chutneys, and spicy condiments. These products are widely consumed due to their rich flavors, long shelf life, and traditional appeal.
The process requires efficient raw material preparation, precise cutting, controlled mixing with spices and oils, and hygienic packaging to ensure consistent taste, preservation, and product quality.

Machines Used:

6. Oil Mixing / Infusion
Oil is blended to enhance preservation and flavor.
👉 Critical for pickle stability and shelf life.

7. Maturation / Curing (VERY IMPORTANT)
Product is allowed to rest for flavor development.
Machines Used:
	•	Storage Tanks / Maturation Tanks 
👉 Ensures:
	•	Proper flavor infusion 
	•	Product stability 

8. Grinding (FOR CHUTNEY PRODUCTS)

Machines Used:
	•	Grinder / Colloid Mill 
👉 Produces smooth or coarse chutney textures.

9. Quality Inspection
Final inspection ensures consistency and quality.

10. Filling & Packaging (RETAIL & BULK)

🏭 H1 HEADING
Complete Dairy Processing Plant & Machinery Solutions (Milk to Value-Added Dairy Products)

🧠 INTRODUCTION (SEO + POSITIONING)
Dairy processing is a highly advanced and essential sector in the global food industry, involving the transformation of raw milk into a wide range of value-added products such as milk, curd, yogurt, butter, ghee, paneer, cheese, milk shakes, buttermilk, lassi, and milk powder.
The process requires strict hygiene standards, temperature-controlled processing, precision equipment, and automated systems to ensure product safety, consistency, and long shelf life.
ART Mechatronics offers complete turnkey dairy processing plant solutions, delivering advanced systems for milk reception, pasteurization, homogenization, fermentation, separation, evaporation, drying, and packaging, designed for large-scale industrial production and global compliance standards.

⚙️ COMMON DAIRY PROCESS (BASE LINE)
1. Milk Reception & Testing
Machines:
	•	Milk Reception Tank 
	•	Weighing System 
	•	Milk Analyzer

Machines:
	•	Milk Filter 
	•	Clarifier 

3. Chilling & Storage
Machines:
	•	Milk Chilling Plant 
	•	Storage Tanks (SS) 

4. Pasteurization (CORE PROCESS)
Machines:
	•	Plate Heat Exchanger (PHE Pasteurizer) 

5. Homogenization
Machines:
	•	Homogenizer 

6. Cream Separation
Machines:
	•	Cream Separator 


🥛 PRODUCT-WISE PROCESS & MACHINERY

🥛 1. MILK (PACKAGED MILK)
Process:
Pasteurization → Homogenization → Cooling → Packaging
Machines:
	•	Pasteurizer 
	•	Homogenizer 
	•	Chilling System 
	•	Milk Storage Tank 
	•	Pouch Packing Machine / Bottle Filling Machine 
👉 Output: Standardized milk (full cream / toned / skimmed)

🍶 2. CURD / YOGURT
Process:
Pasteurization → Cooling → Culture Addition → Incubation → Cooling → Packaging
Machines:
	•	Pasteurizer 
	•	Fermentation Tank 
	•	Incubation Chamber 
	•	Cooling System 
	•	Cup Filling Machine 
👉 Output: Set curd / stirred yogurt

🧈 3. BUTTER
Process:
Cream Separation → Cream Pasteurization → Churning → Washing → Packaging
Machines:
	•	Cream Separator 
	•	Cream Pasteurizer 
	•	Butter Churner 
	•	Butter Working Machine 
	•	Butter Packing Machine 

🧈 4. GHEE

Butter / Cream → Heating → Clarification → Filtration → Packaging
Machines:
	•	Ghee Boiler 
	•	Clarifier 
	•	Filtration Unit 
	•	Storage Tank 
	•	Filling Machine 

🧀 5. PANEER
Process:
Milk Heating → Coagulation → Curd Formation → Pressing → Cutting → Cooling → Packaging
Machines:
	•	Paneer Vat 
	•	Coagulation Tank 
	•	Paneer Press 
	•	Cutting Machine 
	•	Cooling Tank 
	•	Vacuum Packing Machine 

🧀 6. CHEESE
Process:
Milk Standardization → Pasteurization → Culture Addition → Coagulation → Cutting → Whey Removal → Pressing → Ripening → Packaging

	•	Cheese Vat

	•	Cheese Press 
	•	Ripening Chamber 
	•	Cheese Cutting Machine 
	•	Packaging Machine 

🥤 7. MILK SHAKE
Process:
Milk → Mixing with flavor → Homogenization → Pasteurization → Cooling → Packaging
Machines:
	•	Mixing Tank 
	•	Homogenizer 
	•	Pasteurizer 
	•	Bottle Filling Machine 

🥛 8. BUTTERMILK
Process:
Milk / Curd → Dilution → Mixing → Homogenization → Pasteurization → Packaging
Machines:
	•	Mixing Tank 
	•	Homogenizer 
	•	Pasteurizer 
	•	Pouch Packing Machine 

🥛 9. LASSI
Process:
Curd → Mixing with water/sugar → Homogenization → Cooling → Packaging
Machines:
	•	Mixing Tank 
	•	Homogenizer 
	•	Chilling System 
	•	Bottle / Cup Filling Machine 

🥛 10. MILK POWDER
Process:
Milk → Evaporation → Concentration → Spray Drying → Cooling → Packaging
Machines:
	•	Multi Effect Evaporator 
	•	Spray Dryer 
	•	Cyclone Separator 
	•	Fluid Bed Dryer 
	•	Powder Packing Machine 
👉 Output:
	•	Skim Milk Powder 
	•	Whole Milk Powder

The process requires controlled roasting, efficient grinding, precise pressing, and hygienic blending to achieve consistent flavor, color, and texture.
ART Mechatronics provides complete turnkey solutions for cocoa powder and hot chocolate manufacturing plants, offering advanced systems for cocoa processing, powder production, blending, and packaging. Our solutions are designed for high-quality output, consistent formulation, and large-scale industrial operations for domestic and export markets.

1. Raw Cocoa Bean Handling & Cleaning

	•	Pre Cleaner 
	•	Destoner 
	•	Magnetic Separator 
👉 Ensures clean raw material input.

Cocoa beans are roasted to develop flavor and aroma.
Machines Used:
	•	Cocoa Roaster 
👉 Ensures:

	•	Moisture reduction 

3. Cooling
Roasted beans are cooled before further processing.
Machines Used:
	•	Cooling System 

4. Winnowing (Shell Removal)
Shells are removed to obtain cocoa nibs.
Machines Used:
	•	Winnowing Machine 
👉 Produces clean cocoa nibs.

5. Grinding (CORE PROCESS)
Cocoa nibs are ground into cocoa liquor.
Machines Used:
	•	Cocoa Grinder / Ball Mill 
👉 Produces:
	•	Cocoa liquor (paste form) 

6. Pressing (VERY IMPORTANT STAGE)
Cocoa liquor is pressed to separate cocoa butter and cocoa cake.
Machines Used:
	•	Hydraulic Cocoa Press 
👉 Produces:
	•	Cocoa butter 
	•	Cocoa cake 

7. Pulverizing (POWDER FORMATION)
Cocoa cake is ground into fine powder.
Machines Used:
	•	Pulverizer / Pin Mill 
👉 Produces:
	•	Cocoa powder 

8. Alkalization (OPTIONAL – DUTCH PROCESS)
Cocoa powder is treated to modify color and taste.
👉 Produces:
	•	Dark cocoa powder 
	•	Smooth flavor 


☕ HOT CHOCOLATE POWDER PROCESS

Cocoa powder is mixed with sugar, milk powder, and additives.
Machines Used:

👉 Ensures:

	•	Consistent taste

The process requires precise thermal treatment, controlled evaporation, advanced spray drying technology, and hygienic handling systems to ensure consistent quality, solubility, and long shelf life.

⚙️ COMPLETE MILK POWDER PROCESS FLOW

Machines Used:

Machines Used:

4. Standardization & Homogenization
Fat content is adjusted and milk is homogenized.
Machines Used:
	•	Standardization System 
	•	Homogenizer 
👉 Ensures consistent product quality.

5. Evaporation / Concentration (CORE PROCESS)
Milk is concentrated by removing water content.
Machines Used:

👉 Reduces volume and prepares for drying.

Machines Used:

	•	Uniform particle size

Powder is separated from air stream.
Machines Used:
	•	Cyclone Separator 

8. Fluid Bed Drying & Cooling
Powder is further dried and cooled.
Machines Used:
	•	Fluid Bed Dryer 
👉 Ensures:
	•	Proper moisture level 
	•	Improved stability 

9. Agglomeration (OPTIONAL)
Powder is granulated for better solubility.
Machines Used:
	•	Agglomeration System 
👉 Produces instant milk powder.

10. Storage & Handling
Powder is stored in hygienic silos.
Machines Used:
	•	Powder Storage Silo

Machines Used:
	•	Storage Tanks

👉 Ensures precise formulation.

Oils and alkali react to form soap base.
Machines Used:

Moisture is reduced to form solid soap base.
Machines Used:

	•	Amalgamator

	•	Soap Plodder

Bars are cut and branded.
Machines Used:

	•	Stamping Machine 

7. Packaging
Final soap bars are packed.

Machines Used:

👉 Produces base detergent powder.

4. Post Blending
Additional ingredients (enzymes, perfumes) are added.
Machines Used:
	•	Ribbon Blender 

5. Screening & Grading
Powder is classified based on size.
Machines Used:
	•	Vibro Sifter 

6. Packaging (HIGH-SPEED FMCG)
Machines Used:
	•	Pouch Packing Machine 
	•	Bag Filling Machine 
👉 Suitable for:
	•	Retail packs 
	•	Bulk supply

⚙️ COMPLETE PLASTIC RECYCLING PROCESS FLOW

1. Raw Plastic Waste Collection & Feeding
Plastic waste is collected and fed into the system.
Machines Used:
	•	Conveyor System 
	•	Bale Breaker / Feeder 
👉 Ensures continuous material flow.

2. Sorting (CRITICAL STEP)
Different types of plastics are separated.
Machines Used:
	•	Manual Sorting Conveyor 
	•	Automatic Sorting System 
	•	Metal Separator 
👉 Ensures:
	•	Material purity 
	•	Efficient processing 

3. Size Reduction (SHREDDING / CRUSHING)
Machines Used:
	•	Shredder 
	•	Crusher / Granulator 
👉 Produces:
	•	Plastic flakes 

4. Washing & Cleaning (VERY IMPORTANT)
Plastic flakes are cleaned to remove dirt, labels, oil, and contaminants.
Machines Used:
	•	Friction Washer 
	•	Hot Washing System 
	•	Float-Sink Tank 
👉 Ensures clean recycled material.

5. Drying
Moisture is removed from washed plastic.
Machines Used:
	•	Dewatering Machine 
	•	Hot Air Dryer 
👉 Prepares material for extrusion.

6. Agglomeration (FOR FILM PLASTICS)
Machines Used:
	•	Agglomerator 
👉 Converts light films into dense material.

7. Extrusion & Melting (CORE PROCESS)
Plastic is melted and filtered.
Machines Used:
	•	Plastic Extruder 
	•	Melt Filter 
👉 Produces molten plastic.

8. Pelletizing (GRANULE FORMATION)
Molten plastic is converted into granules.
Machines Used:
	•	Pelletizer (Strand / Water Ring / Die Face) 
👉 Produces:
	•	Plastic granules (reusable raw material) 

9. Cooling
Granules are cooled before storage.

10. Storage & Packaging
Final product is packed for sale.
Machines Used:
	•	Storage Silo 
	•	Bag Filling Machine 
👉 Suitable for:
	•	Bulk supply 
	•	Industrial reuse

Biomass & Wood Pellet Manufacturing Plant & Machinery Solutions (Biofuel Pellet Production Systems)

🧠 INTRODUCTION (SEO + POSITIONING)
Pellet manufacturing is a rapidly growing sector in the renewable energy and industrial fuel industry, producing biomass pellets, wood pellets, charcoal pellets, and agro-waste pellets used as eco-friendly fuel alternatives for boilers, furnaces, power plants, and heating systems.
These pellets are made from materials such as wood chips, sawdust, agricultural waste (rice husk, bagasse), charcoal dust, and other biomass residues, converting low-density waste into high-density, energy-efficient fuel.
ART Mechatronics provides complete turnkey solutions for pellet manufacturing plants, offering advanced systems for drying, grinding, pelletizing, cooling, and packaging. Our solutions are designed for high-efficiency production, uniform pellet quality, and scalable operations for domestic and export markets.

⚙️ COMPLETE PELLET MANUFACTURING PROCESS FLOW

	•	Conveyor Systems 
	•	Screw Feeder 
👉 Ensures smooth material flow.

2. Size Reduction (CRITICAL STEP)
Large raw materials are reduced into smaller particles.
Machines Used:
	•	Crusher 
	•	Hammer Mill 
👉 Ensures uniform particle size.

Machines Used:
	•	Pellet Mill (Ring Die / Flat Die) 
👉 Produces:
	•	Uniform pellets 
	•	High-density fuel 

6. Cooling (CRITICAL FOR STRENGTH)
Hot pellets are cooled to stabilize structure.
Machines Used:
	•	Pellet Cooler 
👉 Ensures:
	•	Hardness 
	•	Durability 

7. Screening & Separation
Broken pellets and dust are removed.
Machines Used:
	•	Vibro Sifter / Screening Machine 

8. Dust Collection (IMPORTANT)
Dust generated during grinding and pelletizing is controlled.
Systems Used:
	•	Dust Collector 
	•	Cyclone Separator 
👉 Ensures clean working environment.

9. Storage & Packaging
Finished pellets are stored and packed.
Machines Used:
	•	Storage Silo 
	•	Bag Filling Machine 
👉 Suitable for:
	•	Bulk supply 
	•	Export packaging

Pigment Powder Manufacturing Plant & Machinery | Color Pigment Processing Solutions

🏭 H1 HEADING
Pigment Powder Manufacturing Plant & Machinery Solutions (Color Pigment Production Systems)

🧠 INTRODUCTION (SEO + POSITIONING)
Pigment manufacturing is a critical segment in the chemical industry, producing color pigments used in paints, inks, plastics, coatings, cosmetics, and construction materials. Pigments provide color, opacity, durability, and UV resistance, making them essential across multiple industries.
The manufacturing process requires precise chemical reactions, controlled particle size reduction, uniform dispersion, and high purity levels to achieve consistent color strength and performance.
ART Mechatronics provides complete turnkey solutions for pigment powder manufacturing plants, offering advanced systems for reaction, filtration, drying, grinding, micronizing, blending, and packaging. Our solutions are designed for high-quality pigment production, uniform particle size, and scalable industrial operations.

⚙️ COMPLETE PIGMENT PROCESS FLOW

1. Raw Material Handling & Dosing
Chemical raw materials, intermediates, and additives are received and measured.
Machines Used:
	•	Storage Tanks / Silos 
	•	Dosing System 
👉 Ensures accurate formulation.

2. Chemical Reaction / Synthesis (CORE PROCESS)
Pigments are produced through chemical reactions.
Machines Used:
	•	Chemical Reactor (Jacketed / Stirred) 
👉 Produces pigment slurry.

3. Filtration & Separation (CRITICAL STEP)
Solid pigment is separated from liquid phase.
Machines Used:
	•	Filter Press 
	•	Centrifuge 
👉 Ensures purity of pigment material.

4. Washing
Pigment cake is washed to remove impurities.

5. Drying (VERY IMPORTANT)
Moisture is removed from pigment material.
Machines Used:
	•	Spray Dryer 
	•	Tray Dryer 
	•	Rotary Dryer 
👉 Produces dry pigment material.

6. Grinding / Milling (CORE STAGE)
Pigment particles are reduced to fine size.
Machines Used:
	•	Ball Mill 
	•	Jet Mill 
	•	Pulverizer 
👉 Ensures:
	•	Fine particle size 
	•	Uniform dispersion 

7. Micronizing (HIGH PRECISION STAGE)
Ultra-fine grinding for high-quality pigments.
Machines Used:
	•	Micronizer / Jet Mill 
👉 Produces:
	•	Nano or micro-size particles 

8. Blending & Finishing
Final additives are mixed.
Machines Used:
	•	Ribbon Blender 
👉 Ensures consistency.

9. Classification & Sieving
Particles are classified based on size.
Machines Used:
	•	Vibro Sifter 

10. Quality Testing
Pigments are tested for:
	•	Color strength 
	•	Particle size 
	•	Purity 

11. Packaging
Final product is packed.
Machines Used:
	•	Bag Filling Machine 
	•	Pouch Packing Machine 
👉 Suitable for:
	•	Industrial bulk supply 

🎨 TYPES OF PIGMENTS PRODUCED (SEO BOOST)
	•	Organic Pigments 
	•	Inorganic Pigments 
	•	Iron Oxide Pigments 
	•	Titanium Dioxide (TiO₂) 
	•	Color Pigments for Paints 
	•	Pigments for Plastics & Masterbatch 
	•	Cosmetic Grade Pigments

	•	Storage Tanks / Silos

	•	Chemical Reactor

ART Mechatronics provides complete turnkey solutions for industrial dust collection and pollution control systems, offering advanced equipment such as bag house dust collectors, cartridge filters, cyclone separators, wet scrubbers, and centralized de-dusting systems designed for high efficiency, minimal maintenance, and long-term reliability.

👉 Best for:
	•	Fine powder industries 

🔹 Cyclone Separator
	•	Uses centrifugal force 
👉 Pre-separation of heavy particles

	•	Wet Scrubber 
👉 Ensures:

Collected dust is discharged safely.
Systems Used:
	•	Rotary Airlock Valve 
	•	Dust Collection Hopper

Clean air is released back into environment.
👉 Ensures compliance with emission norms.

35) PACKAGING 

PACKAGING – 

🔥 SEO TITLE (Meta Title)
Packaging Machines & Automation Systems | Pouch, Bottle, Bag, Carton & Industrial Packaging Solutions

🔥 META DESCRIPTION
Complete packaging machine solutions including FFS, multihead, auger, pouch, bottle, carton, vacuum, and secondary packaging systems. Turnkey automation for all industries.

🏭 H1 HEADING
Packaging Machines & Automation Systems (Pouch, Packet, Bag, Bottle, Can & Carton Packaging Solutions)

🧠 INTRODUCTION (SEO + POSITIONING)
Packaging is a critical stage in every manufacturing process, ensuring product safety, shelf life, branding, and efficient distribution. Industries such as FMCG, food, agro, pharma, chemicals, dairy, snacks, powders, liquids, and industrial goods rely on advanced packaging systems for high-speed and accurate operations.
Modern packaging requires precision filling, airtight sealing, automated handling, coding, inspection, and secondary packaging systems to meet market standards and regulatory requirements.
ART Mechatronics provides complete turnkey packaging solutions, offering advanced machines for pouch, packet, bag, sack, bottle, can, and carton packaging, designed for high-speed, accuracy, flexibility, and seamless plant integration.

⚙️ COMPLETE PACKAGING PROCESS FLOW

1. Product Feeding & Dosing (CRITICAL STEP)
Products are accurately measured before packaging.

Filling Technologies:
	•	🔹 Multihead Weigher → snacks, dry products 
	•	🔹 Auger Filler → powders 
	•	🔹 Cup Filler → granules 
	•	🔹 Liquid Filling System → liquids 
👉 Ensures:
	•	Accurate weight 
	•	Consistent filling 

2. Primary Packaging (CORE PROCESS)

🔸 Form Fill Seal (FFS) Machines
	•	Vertical & Horizontal FFS 

🔸 Collar Type Packing Machine
	•	For pouches & packets 

🔸 Rotary Packing Machine
	•	High-speed pouch packaging 

🔸 Pneumatic / Mechanical / Servo Packaging Machines
	•	Based on automation level 
👉 Produces:
	•	Pouches 
	•	Packets 

3. Specialized Packaging Systems

🛍️ Flow Wrap Machine
	•	For bars, biscuits, bakery 

🔥 Heat Shrink Packaging
	•	For bundled products 

🧊 Vacuum Packaging
	•	For extended shelf life 

🔒 Band Sealer
	•	For pouch sealing 

4. Container Packaging

🧴 Bottle Filling & Labeling
	•	Bottle filling machine 
	•	Labeling machine 

🥫 Tin / Can Packaging
	•	Tin can filling 
	•	Can seaming machine 


5. Secondary Packaging (VERY IMPORTANT)

📦 Carton Handling:
	•	Carton Taping Machine 
	•	Carton Stretch Wrapping 
	•	Carton Strapping Machine 

🧵 Bag & Sack Handling:
	•	Bag Filling Machine 
	•	Sack Filling Machine 
	•	Bag Stitching Machine 

👉 Ensures:
	•	Safe transport 
	•	Bulk handling 

6. Coding & Inspection

	•	Prints MRP, batch, date 

⚖️ Checkweigher
	•	Ensures correct weight 

👉 Ensures compliance & quality control.

2.1  Company Story — Managing Director's Message

Body copy (suggested):
ART Mechatronics was founded on a single conviction: that Indian manufacturers deserve world-class processing machinery at fair prices, backed by genuine after-sales support. Over 15 years, we have grown from a specialist dryer and mixer manufacturer into a full-spectrum industrial machinery company serving 32 industries across 20+ countries.
Our journey has been defined by close partnerships with our clients — understanding their process, their constraints, and their growth ambitions — and engineering solutions that deliver results, not just equipment. Today, with offices in India, UAE, and Thailand, and service teams deployed across both regions, we are positioned to serve as the industrial machinery partner of choice for manufacturers across Asia, the Middle East, and beyond.

5. Turnkey Plant Solutions

Headline: 'From Empty Floor to Full Production — ART Mechatronics Delivers Complete Turnkey Plants'

Sub-copy: We don't just supply machines — we design, supply, install, commission and hand over complete processing plants. Our turnkey service covers plant layout, utility planning, machine selection, civil coordination, installation, trial runs, and operator training.

5.1  Turnkey Process
Step
Phase
What We Do
1
Consultation
Understand production requirement, capacity, product spec, budget and site constraints
2
Process Design
Develop process flow diagram (PFD), equipment list, layout drawing (AutoCAD)
3
Proposal
Detailed techno-commercial proposal with machine specs, delivery, payment terms, warranty
4
Manufacturing
Manufacture, fabricate and source all equipment to approved specs
5
Factory Testing
Full FAT (Factory Acceptance Test) before dispatch — client witness optional
6
Logistics
Coordinate packing, freight, customs and site delivery
7
Installation
Our engineers install and align all equipment on-site
8
Commissioning
Trial production runs, parameter optimisation, punch list clearance
9
Training
Operator and maintenance staff training — typically 2–5 days on-site
10
Handover & AMC
Formal handover with documentation; Annual Maintenance Contract offered

7. Innovation & Technology

Headline: 'Engineering Tomorrow's Solutions — Today'

7.1  R&D Focus Areas
	•	Energy-efficient dryer and heater designs — targeting 25–35% lower power consumption
	•	PLC-based automation across standard product range — replacing manual controls
	•	IoT-ready sensor integration — temperature, moisture, vibration monitoring on key machines
	•	Food-safety by design — hygienic machine architecture, CIP-compatible surfaces
	•	Modular machine design — quick changeover for multi-product facilities

7.2  Automation & Smart Controls
	•	Standard PLC (Siemens / Allen Bradley / Mitsubishi) integration on dryers, mixers, packaging lines
	•	HMI (Human Machine Interface) touchscreen panels — intuitive operation
	•	SCADA integration available for full plant monitoring
	•	VFD (Variable Frequency Drive) on all motors — energy savings + soft start
	•	Recipe management — save, recall, and lock product-specific parameters
	•	Remote monitoring capability — production data accessible via laptop/mobile

7.3  Food Safety & Hygiene Engineering
	•	SS 304 / SS 316 product-contact surfaces — standard on food-grade machines
	•	No dead zones, radius corners, and easy-access panels for cleaning
	•	FSSAI-compatible design philosophy
	•	CIP (Clean-In-Place) options for closed-system applications
	•	IP65-rated electrical panels for dusty environments

7.4  Patented Technology

8. Sustainability & ESG

Headline: 'Machinery That Works Harder — For You and the Planet'

8.1  Energy Efficiency
	•	All ART dryers designed with insulated chambers and heat recovery systems
	•	VFD installed on all motors as standard — proven energy savings of 15–30%
	•	Thermic fluid heating systems used in preference to direct steam where possible — higher efficiency, lower emissions
	•	Target: every machine redesign must achieve at least 10% energy reduction vs. predecessor model

8.2  Waste Reduction
	•	Dust collection systems supplied with every grinding and milling system — zero product loss to atmosphere
	•	Recyclable packaging for all spare parts shipments
	•	Modular machine design extends product lifecycle — reduces need for full machine replacement

8.3  Make in India — Local Value Creation
	•	95%+ of components sourced from Indian suppliers — supporting domestic manufacturing ecosystem
	•	Reducing India's dependence on imported processing machinery
	•	Export earnings from 20+ countries bring foreign exchange to India

8.4  Our Sustainability Commitments
	•	By 2026: 100% of new machine designs to include energy metering as standard
	•	By 2027: Carbon footprint baseline measured for our manufacturing facility
	•	By 2028: Offer verifiable sustainability data sheet for every machine category
